{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Design a \"peak-innovative\" workstream for SIGIL (a local-first, OFFENSE-FREE-by-doctrine personal AI orchestrator at /home/kali/sigil): an OWNER-CONSENTED identity/account manager + autonomous web ACTOR. SIGIL holds the owner's OWN email/username/password (a vault) and, WITH PER-ACTION owner approval, creates accounts, logs in, fills forms, and performs advanced web tasks on the owner's behalf — using an HTTP-FIRST + headless-browser-FALLBACK engine. Strict doctrine: offense-free; the owner's OWN identity only; third parties are off-limits to attack (test only the owner's integration); NO CAPTCHA/bot-detection EVASION (if a site blocks automation, SURFACE it as a positive control — never defeat it); NO mass account creation; NO impersonation; every account/login/submit/purchase action is A3 (explicit, per-action, owner-signed, NO promotion — like ENVOY); credentials NEVER touch the append-only spine. Phase cadence: reuse-first → deterministic tests → real proof → red-pen → fix → PR.\n\nSUBSTRATE (use this; do NOT re-explore):\n- CREDENTIALS: sigil/platform/secrets.py SecretStore (keyring-first, sigil.env fallback, get/set, 0600 up-front via os.open) — invariant: \"Secrets NEVER enter the append-only spine, a log, or a network payload.\" Only SCALAR secrets today (no per-service username/password record type). sigil/config.py _load_env_file/SIGIL_HOME. The 0700-JOURNAL pattern (operator.py:205-212: executable plan WITH content → 0700 journal; spine gets HASHES ONLY) is the established way to keep bytes off the append-only spine.\n- WEB FETCH (the HTTP path): sigil/agents/sources.py read_source(ref) is SSRF-gated (resolve-once _vetted_ip, verify-all-public, PIN the IP _PinnedHTTP(S)Connection keeping SNI/cert on the real host, _NoRedirect refuses 3xx, HTML strip), fail-closed to \"\". Stable correlatable UA \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\". consolidate/extract.py ApiProvider = raw urllib POST template. NO requests/httpx/selenium/playwright/bs4 — greenfield; stdlib urllib only.\n- HUMAN-GATE PATTERN: sigil/agents/approvals.py ApprovalQueue.approve/deny (owner Ed25519-signed, verify_approval fail-closed, extra_pubkeys=authorized-device set). sigil/perception/egress.py is the EXACT pattern to mirror: egress_token = sha256(subject|bound-fields) binds an approval to ONE action; egress_approved requires a VERIFIED owner/device approval bound to that token before acting; classify → queue A2/A3 → withhold-until-approved. sigil/agents/envoy.py = NO send()/transmit() EVER (structural), ceiling A2 hard; governor/promotion.py NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS = {ENVOY} (fail-closed, forged grants ignored) — the template for a permanently-human-gated agent.\n- GATED EXECUTOR: sigil/agents/operator.py plan→PREVIEW(no mutation)→hash-bound APPROVE→execute→VERIFY(re-read the world)→ROLLBACK/undo; anti-TOCTOU (re-derive tier from hash-bound preview, never a stored field); OperatorScope (read/auto-write ring allowlist, empty=deny-all) = the \"which services may SIGIL act on\" scope-gate model.\n- WARDEN ORACLE (already enforces the doctrine — VERIFIED by running it): login, account.create/login, credential.read, password.autofill, form.fill, submit.form, browser.navigate, purchase → ALL A3 (explicit, per-action, no-promotion). email.send/message.send → A2. http.get → A0 but web.fetch/scrape.fetch → A3. So if you name tools honestly, WARDEN already forces every account/login/submit/purchase to A3-signed-no-promotion. kernel_classify.py bridges it (fail-closed A3).\n- DOCTRINE (honor verbatim): CLAUDE.md §II (third parties off-limits, test only the owner's integration), §VI (throttle, correlatable UA, WAF=positive-control don't evade), §VIII (ask before touching real payment/destruction). SIGIL.md §12 (no offensive capability, no covert surveillance of others, no WARDEN-bypass), §4.6 ENVOY (A2 hard, no promotion, human-gated forever), C32 (form-fill browser automation, writes A2 — spec-only), C33 (outbound payment A3). assert_no_offense() blocks framework.* imports.\n\nDesign a DETAILED plan. Produce:\n1. PEAK-INNOVATIVE mechanisms — advanced, doctrine-safe: (a) a per-service CREDENTIAL VAULT extending SecretStore (a {service, email, username, password-ref, notes} record type; password in keyring; the vault manifest carries NO passwords; credentials resolved only at execute time, never on the spine, never in a Proposal payload — reuse the 0700-journal discipline); (b) a WEB ACTOR: given a task, PLAN a sequence of web steps (navigate/fill/submit/create-account/login), PREVIEW them (which fields, which vault data by REFERENCE not value, a dry-run) exactly like Operator preview, then execute ONLY after a per-action A3 owner-signed approval bound to an action-token (mirror egress.py); (c) HTTP-FIRST engine on read_source's SSRF-gated + pinned + correlatable-UA foundation for simple/static flows, escalating to a HEADLESS BROWSER provider (lazy-imported Playwright behind a WebEngine Protocol + a deterministic LOCAL-test-server double) only when a page needs JS; (d) STRUCTURAL offense-free guards: a NO_PROMOTION identity agent (ENVOY model); a \"bot-block = positive control\" detector that, on a CAPTCHA/403/anti-automation signal, STOPS and surfaces it (never a bypass); a per-service creation CAP; owner-identity-only (no arbitrary-identity field); (e) every credential-touching action keeps secrets off-spine (spine logs the action + a field-hash + the gated decision, never the value).\n2. Ordered, independently-mergeable SLICES naming files to create/modify + exact reuse points.\n3. Test + real-proof strategy (deterministic doubles: a LOCAL test HTTP server with a fake login/signup form; a WebEngine double; assert secrets never appear in the spine/logs; assert an unapproved action performs NO request; assert a CAPTCHA/403 STOPS + surfaces) + red-pen lenses (credential leak to spine/log/UA/referrer; an action firing without a verified approval; approval replay across actions/services; evasion drift; third-party attack; the browser provider exfiltrating; impersonation via an identity field).\n4. Doctrine-safety analysis + the exact abuse vectors a reviewer will hunt, and how each is structurally prevented.\n5. How the HTTP-first/browser-fallback escalation decision is made + kept honest.\nDo not write code; design + enumerate. Return a thorough structured document."},"uuid":"20fbaefa-860b-453e-ac62-4d6b807e6a3b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:29:40.693Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"944b8a78-fc48-45fa-bce4-30e42c2b5527","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":19,"isInitial":true,"names":["crucible","deep-research","adversarial-sweep","dataviz","artifact-design","artifact-capabilities","update-config","keybindings-help","verify","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","schedule","claude-api","run","init","review","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"dee6738d-5349-43cc-a0c2-642ea74ca7e2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:29:40.697Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ad29990d-f81a-476f-bd7c-d6c9618575bf","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VgbpGycCWbyKdrkFJ5V2ZG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SecretStore (Phase 7, WS-D D-ii) — keyring-first secret handling. Reads/writes API keys via the\n2\tOS keyring (macOS Keychain / Windows Credential Manager / libsecret) when the `keyring` package is\n3\tpresent; otherwise falls back to the plaintext `~/.sigil/sigil.env` that `config._load_env_file`\n4\talready loads. Secrets NEVER enter the append-only spine, a log, or a network payload — this is\n5\towner-local plumbing that keeps them off the plaintext file and off the wire (WS-D transport carries\n6\tonly `{seq,tier,kind}`).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport os\n10\tfrom typing import Optional\n11\t\n12\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n13\t\n14\t_SERVICE = \"sigil\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tclass SecretStore:\n18\t    def __init__(self):\n19\t        try:\n20\t            import keyring\n21\t            self._kr = keyring\n22\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no keyring backend → env fallback only\n23\t            self._kr = None\n24\t\n25\t    @property\n26\t    def backend(self) -> str:\n27\t        return \"keyring\" if self._kr is not None else \"envfile\"\n28\t\n29\t    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[str]:\n30\t        if self._kr is not None:\n31\t            try:\n32\t                v = self._kr.get_password(_SERVICE, key)\n33\t                if v is not None:\n34\t                    return v\n35\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n36\t                pass\n37\t        return os.environ.get(key)                    # sigil.env is loaded into env at config import\n38\t\n39\t    def set(self, key: str, value: str) -> str:\n40\t        \"\"\"Store a secret. Returns the backend used. Prefers the keyring; falls back to sigil.env\n41\t        (0600). Never writes the value anywhere else.\"\"\"\n42\t        if self._kr is not None:\n43\t            try:\n44\t                self._kr.set_password(_SERVICE, key, value)\n45\t                os.environ[key] = value               # make it live this process too\n46\t                return \"keyring\"\n47\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n48\t                pass\n49\t        self._env_upsert(key, value)\n50\t        os.environ[key] = value\n51\t        return \"envfile\"\n52\t\n53\t    @staticmethod\n54\t    def _env_upsert(key: str, value: str) -> None:\n55\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n56\t        SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n57\t        lines = []\n58\t        found = False\n59\t        try:\n60\t            for ln in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n61\t                if ln.split(\"=\", 1)[0].strip() == key:\n62\t                    lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\"); found = True\n63\t                else:\n64\t                    lines.append(ln)\n65\t        except OSError:\n66\t            pass\n67\t        if not found:\n68\t            lines.append(f\"{key}={value}\")\n69\t        # create with 0600 up-front (no world-readable window on first write) — N4\n70\t        fd = os.open(str(f), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)\n71\t        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n72\t            fh.write(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n73\t"}]},"uuid":"366188d1-0e11-4cf8-ab69-a67bd33ed3ca","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:29:53.173Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ad29990d-f81a-476f-bd7c-d6c9618575bf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"0565b3ba-efb0-4ca3-b6fd-f952151b14e7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VNayfSWEpA6rrrGDTfuBUc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Frontier-vision EGRESS gate (Phase 7, WS-A V-ii). Sending a screen/camera frame to the frontier\n2\tAnthropic VLM uploads private bytes off the owned machine — that is A2 data-egress, not a free\n3\t\"try local then frontier\" quality bump. This gate makes the cascade doctrine-honest:\n4\t\n5\t  • The tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`KernelClassifier` on `vision.frontier.upload` → A2),\n6\t    never self-declared.\n7\t  • Nothing uploads until a VERIFIED owner approval exists that is BOUND to this exact egress — an\n8\t    `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256 | question)`. An approval of a different egress (a replay)\n9\t    does not match `target_seq` and does not authorize the upload.\n10\t  • Absent approval, the egress is QUEUED (an A2 proposal recorded on the spine) and the frontier\n11\t    reading is WITHHELD; the local (on-box) reading still stands.\n12\t\n13\tReuses the hardened signed `ApprovalQueue` verification (`verify_approval`) — the same fail-closed\n14\towner-key check the mesh already trusts.\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\tEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\n22\tFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n26\t    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n27\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n31\t    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED approval by the\n32\t    owner OR an owner-authorized device. Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an\n33\t    approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n34\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n35\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n36\t        return False\n37\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n38\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n39\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n40\t        p = r.payload\n41\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n42\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n43\t            return True\n44\t    return False\n45\t"}]},"uuid":"5a539151-957f-4151-bd4d-d06a62ed3f59","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:29:53.359Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0565b3ba-efb0-4ca3-b6fd-f952151b14e7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Empty rings = deny-all.\n10\t  • PREVIEW mutates NOTHING; the approval BINDS to the previewed content hash — before executing, the\n11\t    Operator re-previews the CURRENT files and aborts on any mismatch (anti-TOCTOU).\n12\t  • the executable plan (with file contents) lives in a 0700 journal, NOT the append-only spine — no\n13\t    file bytes leak into immutable memory; the spine record carries only hashes.\n14\t  • EXECUTE journals a pre-image (bytes + mode) before each mutating step, applies atomically,\n15\t    RESTORES the original mode, and VERIFIES by re-reading the post-image hash.\n16\t  • ROLLBACK restores every applied step in reverse and is HONEST about a restore that failed or an\n17\t    irreversible `shell` that already ran. .operator_scope import OperatorScope\n35\t\n36\t_OP_HOME = SIGIL_HOME / \"operator\"\n37\t_JOURNAL = _OP_HOME / \"journal\"          # per-execute pre-images\n38\t_PLANS = _OP_HOME / \"plans\"              # executable plans (with content) — off the spine\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef _hash_bytes(b: bytes) -> str:\n42\t    return sha256_hex(b)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef _secure(d: Path) -> None:\n46\t    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n47\t    try:\n48\t        os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n49\t    except OSError:\n50\t        pass\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n54\tclass Step:\n55\t    op: str reason: str = \"\"\n89\t    plan_seq: Optional[int] = None\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tclass Operator(Agent):\n93\t    name = \"OPERATOR\"\n94\t    mandate = \"open files + run commands on request; tiered, trusted_pubkey\n103\t\n104\t    def _cls(self):\n105\t        if self._classifier is None:\n106\t            from .kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n107\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n108\t        return self._classifier\n109\t\n110\t    def _tp(self):\n111\t        if self._trusted_pubkey is None:\n112\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n113\t            self._trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()\n114\t        return self._trusted_pubkey\n115\t\n116\t    # --- B3: grounded read (hardlink-guarded) -----------------------------------------------------\n117\t    def read(self, path: str, question: str = \"\") -> AgentResult:\n118\t        rp = self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\")\n119\t        # BLOCK-1: a regular file with >1 hardlink cannot be confined to the ring by path (a link\n120\t        # inside the root may alias an out-of-scope inode) → refuse, fail-closed.\n121\t        if rp is not None and rp.is_file() and rp.stat().st_nlink > 1:\n122\t            rp = None\n123\t        if rp is None:\n124\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n125\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": path,\n126\t                                       \"reason\": \"path is in_scope = self.scope.in_read(step.cwd) if step.cwd else True\n152\t            detail = \"argv: \" + \" \".join(step.argv or []) + (f\"  (cwd {step.cwd})\" if step.cwd else \"\")\n153\t False, \"path left scope at execute time\", None\n305\t        pre = rp.read_bytes() if rp.exists() else None\n306\t        pre_mode = (rp.stat().st_mode & 0o777) if rp.exists() else None\n307\t        if pre is not None:\n308\t            (jdir / f\"{i}.pre\").write_bytes(pre)                    # journal pre-image bytes...\n309\t        if step.op == \"write\":\n310\t            new = (step.content or \"\").encode(\"utf-8\")\n311\t            fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=str(rp.parent), prefix=\".op-\")\n312\t            try:\n313\t                with os.fdopen(fd, \"wb\") as f:\n314\t                    f.write(new)\n315\t                os.replace(tmp, rp)                                  # atomic\n316\t            except OSError as e:\n317\t                Path(tmp).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n318\t                return False, f\"write failed: {e}\", None\n319\t            if pre_mode is not None:\n320\t                try:\n321\t                    os.chmod(rp, pre_mode)                           # BLOCK-4: preserve the original mode\n322\t                except OSError:\n323\t                    pass\n324\t            if _hash_bytes(rp.read_bytes()) != pv.post_hash:        # VERIFY by re-reading the world\n325\t                return False, \"post-image hash mismatch after write\", None\n326\t            inv = {\"op\": \"restore\" if pre is not None else \"delete-created\", \"path\": str(rp),\n327\t                   \"pre\": str(jdir / f\"{i}.pre\") if pre is not None else None, \"pre_mode\": pre_mode,\n328\t                   \"post_hash\": pv.post_hash}\n329\t            return True, \"write applied + verified\", inv\n330\t        if step.op == \"delete\":\n331\t            if pre is None:\n332\t                return True, \"delete: target already absent\", None\n333\t            try:\n334\t                rp.unlink()\n335\t            except OSError as e:\n336\t                return False, f\"delete failed: {e}\", None\n337\t            if rp.exists():\n338\t                return False, \"post-image mismatch: file still present after delete\", None\n339\t            return True, \"delete applied + verified\", {\"op\": \"restore\", \"path\": str(rp),\n340\t                                                       \"pre\": str(jdir / f\"{i}.pre\"), \"pre_mode\": pre_mode,\n341\t                                                       \"post_hash\": None}\n342\t        return False, f\"unknown op {step.op}\", None\n343\t\n344\t    def _restore_one(self, inv: dict, *, verify_unchanged: bool) -> tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:\n345\t        \"\"\"Restore a single inverse. Returns (restored, skip_reason). `verify_unchanged` (undo) refuses\n346\t        to clobber a file the owner changed since (hash-bind), and re-resolves the path through scope.\"\"\"\n347\t        path = inv.get(\"path\")\n348\t        if verify_unchanged:\n349\t            if path is None or self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\") is None:\n350\t                return False, f\"{path}: left scope — not restored\"\n351\t            cur = Path(path)\n352\t            cur_hash = _hash_bytes(cur.read_bytes()) if cur.exists() else None\n353\t            if inv.get(\"post_hash\") is not None and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n354\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — NOT clobbered\"\n355\t            if inv.get(\"op\") == \"delete-created\" and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n356\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — not deleted\"\n357\t        try:\n358\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"restore\":\n359\t                shutil.copyfile(inv[\"pre\"], path)\n360\t                if inv.get(\"pre_mode\") is not None:\n361\t                    os.chmod(path, inv[\"pre_mode\"])                 # BLOCK-3: restore original mode\n362\t            elif inv[\"op\"] == \"delete-created\":\n363\t                Path(path).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n364\t            else:\n365\t                return False, None                                  # irreversible — handled by caller\n366\t            return True, None\n367\t        except OSError as e:\n368\t            return False, f\"{path}: restore FAILED ({e})\"\n369\t\n370\t    def _rollback(self, applied: List[dict], *, verify_unchanged: bool = False) -> tuple[int, str]:\n371\t        n, failed, irreversible = 0, [], []\n372\t        for inv in reversed(applied):\n373\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"irreversible\":\n374\t                irreversible.append(inv.get(\"step\"))\n375\t                continue\n376\t            ok, why = self._restore_one(inv, verify_unchanged=verify_unchanged)\n377\t            if ok:\n378\t                n += 1\n379\t            elif why:\n380\t                failed.append(why)                                  # BLOCK-5: never call a failed restore clean\n381\t        parts = []\n382\t        if failed:\n383\t            parts.append(\"PARTIAL — not restored: \" + \"; \".join(failed))\n384\t        if irreversible:\n385\t            parts.append(f\"step(s) {irreversible} IRREVERSIBLE (shell already ran)\")\n386\t        return n, (\"; \".join(parts) if parts else \"clean rollback\")\n387\t\n388\t    def undo(self, execute_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n389\t        rec = self.store.get(execute_seq)\n390\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n391\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != \"operator.execute\" or rec.payload.get(\"status\") != \"APPLIED\":\n392\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {execute_seq} is not an applied operator execution\")\n393\t            return res\n394\t        # BLOCK-6: single-shot — refuse a second undo of the same execution.\n395\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=execute_seq):\n396\t            if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"operator.undo\" and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == execute_seq:\n397\t                res.notes.append(f\"execute {execute_seq} was already undone (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay\")\n398\t                return res\n399\t        n, note = self._rollback(rec.payload.get(\"inverses\", []), verify_unchanged=True)  # hash-bound, scope-checked\n400\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"operation\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n401\t                                payload={\"signal\": \"operator.undo\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": \"A1\",\n402\t                                         \"target_seq\": execute_seq, \"reverted\": n, \"note\": note,\n403\t                                         \"summary\": f\"undo of execute {execute_seq}: reverted {n} step(s) — {note}\"},\n404\t                                supersedes_id=execute_seq)\n405\t        res.applied.append(seq)\n406\t        res.notes.append(f\"undo of {execute_seq}: reverted {n} step(s) — {note}\")\n407\t        return res\n408\t"}]},"uuid":"dc7875d1-a497-45ee-b0d3-b8743dfa10eb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:20.798Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f517d0a0-8bc0-4946-89db-e7980d530c0f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3f03c9fb-d5bd-4f34-a10e-ce1b3a9a3a32","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FxbZ3g2C5SEBiuDndGRrLo","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Source reading for SCHOLAR — a URL (web fetch, HTML stripped) or a local file/doc path → text.\n2\tKept small + dependency-free (urllib + a crude tag strip); a richer extractor is a later add.\n3\t\n4\tSSRF GATE (Phase 6, closes the carried debt + red-pen SSRF-REBIND-TOCTOU): the host is resolved\n5\tONCE; every resolved address must be public (not private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n6\tunspecified — IPv4 and IPv6, incl. IPv4-mapped IPv6); and the connection is then PINNED to that exact\n7\tvetted IP, so a rebinding DNS that flips to an internal address between the check and the fetch cannot\n8\tsteer SCHOLAR onto `169.254.169.254`/`localhost`/an internal service. Redirects are not followed. A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.request\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import Optional\n19\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n20\t\n21\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n22\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n23\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n27\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n28\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n32\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n33\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n34\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n35\t    if not host:\n36\t        return None\n37\t    try:\n38\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n39\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n40\t        return None\n41\t    if not infos:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    chosen = None\n44\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n45\t        try:\n46\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n47\t        except ValueError:\n48\t            return None\n49\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n50\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n51\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n52\t        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved\n53\t                or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified):\n54\t            return None\n55\t        if chosen is None:\n56\t            chosen = sockaddr[0]\n57\t    return chosen\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef is_public_host(host: str) -> bool:\n61\t    return _vetted_ip(host) is not None\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tclass _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n65\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n66\t\n67\t    def connect(self):\n68\t        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n69\t                                              self.timeout, self.source_address)\n70\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n71\t            self._tunnel()\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tclass _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):\n75\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n76\t\n77\t    def connect(self):\n78\t        sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n79\t                                        self.timeout, self.source_address)\n80\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n81\t            self.sock = sock\n82\t            self._tunnel()\n83\t            sock = self.sock\n84\t        # SNI + certificate validation still use the ORIGINAL hostname, not the pinned IP.\n85\t        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tclass _PinnedHTTPHandler(urllib.request.HTTPHandler):\n89\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n90\t        super().__init__()\n91\t        self._ip = ip\n92\t\n93\t    def http_open(self, req):\n94\t        ip = self._ip\n95\t\n96\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n97\t            c = _PinnedHTTPConnection(host, **kw)\n98\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n99\t            return c\n100\t        return self.do_open(factory, req)\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tclass _PinnedHTTPSHandler(urllib.request.HTTPSHandler):\n104\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n105\t        super().__init__()\n106\t        self._ip = ip\n107\t\n108\t    def https_open(self, req):\n109\t        ip = self._ip\n110\t\n111\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n112\t            c = _PinnedHTTPSConnection(host, **kw)\n113\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n114\t            return c\n115\t        return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n119\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not redirect SCHOLAR onto an internal host.\"\"\"\n120\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n121\t        return None\n122\t\n123\t\n124\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n125\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (resolve-once, verify-all-public,\n126\t    pin the IP, no redirects) then fetched + HTML-stripped; anything else is a local path. Never\n127\t    raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n128\t    try:\n129\t        if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n130\t            host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n131\t            ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n132\t            if ip is None:\n133\t                return \"\"       # SSRF gate: non-public / unresolvable → no request leaves the machine\n134\t            opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n135\t            req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"})\n136\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n137\t                raw = r.read().decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n138\t            return _strip_html(raw)[:max_chars]\n139\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n140\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n141\t        return \"\"\n142\t"}]},"uuid":"d9ab177f-a35b-4860-a5ed-3641e9451c8d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:21.272Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3f03c9fb-d5bd-4f34-a10e-ce1b3a9a3a32","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"99c31e94-d898-4588-889c-4e5b2b8f1d22","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FdpR1Fys6HBDokoHCNnC13","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ENVOY (SIGIL §4.6) — communications & relationships: triage inbound, DRAFT outbound, track\n2\topen loops. Ceiling A2 HARD, and NO PROMOTION PATH — outbound stays human-gated permanently.\n3\t\n4\tThis is enforced STRUCTURALLY: ENVOY has no method that transmits anything. It only classifies\n5\tinbound and writes `draft` records (A2 → queued, `status: awaiting-approval`). A human reads the\n6\tdraft and sends it (or not). There is deliberately no `send()`. The inbox is a pluggable source\n7\t(a file of messages for tests/offline; IMAP is an optional real source, not built here).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\tfrom pathlib import Path\n12\tfrom typing import List, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n13\t\n14\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n15\t\n16\t_SPAM = (\"unsubscribe\", \"viagra\", \"lottery\", \"prince\", \"crypto giveaway\", \"you have won\", \"click here to claim\")\n17\t_URGENT = (\"urgent\", \"asap\", \"as soon as possible\", \"deadline\", \"today\", \"emergency\", \"overdue\", \"past due\", \"final notice\")\n18\t_FYI = (\"no-reply\", \"noreply\", \"newsletter\", \"notification\", \"digest\", \"do not reply\")\n19\t\n20\t\n21\t@runtime_checkable\n22\tclass InboxSource(Protocol):\n23\t    def messages(self) -> List[dict]: ...   # each: {from, subject, body, date}\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tclass FileInbox:\n27\t    \"\"\"A JSON file of messages — for tests/offline. Real IMAP is a separate optional source.\"\"\"\n28\t    def __init__(self, path: str):\n29\t        self.path = Path(path)\n30\t\n31\t    def messages(self) -> List[dict]:\n32\t        if not self.path.exists():\n33\t            return []\n34\t        try:\n35\t            data = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n36\t        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):\n37\t            return []   # a corrupt/truncated inbox degrades to empty, never crashes the triage pass\n38\t        return data if isinstance(data, list) else data.get(\"messages\", [])\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef triage(msg: dict) -> str:\n42\t    \"\"\"Classify a message → urgent | normal | fyi | spam (deterministic, keyword-based).\"\"\"\n43\t    sender = str(msg.get(\"from\", \"\")).lower()\n44\t    text = f\"{msg.get('subject', '')} {msg.get('body', '')}\".lower()\n45\t    if any(k in text for k in _SPAM):\n46\t        return \"spam\"\n47\t    if any(k in sender for k in _FYI):\n48\t        return \"fyi\"\n49\t    if any(k in text for k in _URGENT):\n50\t        return \"urgent\"\n51\t    return \"normal\"\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tdef draft_reply(msg: dict) -> str:\n55\t    \"\"\"A conservative acknowledgement DRAFT (never sent). A real deployment can swap in an\n56\t    agent-composed draft via the cognition cascade; the doctrine (drafts-only) is unchanged.\"\"\"\n57\t    who = str(msg.get(\"from\", \"there\")).split(\"@\")[0].split(\"<\")[0].strip() or \"there\"\n58\t    subj = msg.get(\"subject\", \"your message\")\n59\t    return (f\"Hi {who},\\n\\nThanks for your message regarding \\\"{subj}\\\" — I've received it and \"\n60\t            f\"will follow up shortly.\\n\\n[DRAFT — SIGIL/ENVOY. Review and send manually; nothing is sent automatically.]\")\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tclass Envoy(Agent):\n64\t    name = \"ENVOY\"\n65\t    mandate = \"triage inbound, DRAFT outbound (never send), track open loops\"\n66\t    ceiling = Tier.A2  # hard — no promotion to auto, ever\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self, inbox: InboxSource) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        proposals: List[Proposal] = []\n70\t        open_loops = 0\n71\t        for msg in inbox.messages():\n72\t            cls = triage(msg)\n73\t            # the triage note is a reversible internal record → A1, auto.\n74\t            proposals.append(Proposal(\"interaction\", {\n75\t                \"from\": msg.get(\"from\"), \"subject\": msg.get(\"subject\"),\n76\t                \"triage\": cls, \"date\": msg.get(\"date\")}, Tier.A1))\n77\t            if cls in (\"urgent\", \"normal\"):\n78\t                open_loops += 1\n79\t                # the reply is a DRAFT — A2, ALWAYS queued (never auto-sent).\n80\t                proposals.append(Proposal(\"draft\", {\n81\t                    \"to\": msg.get(\"from\"), \"subject\": f\"Re: {msg.get('subject', '')}\",\n82\t                    \"body\": draft_reply(msg), \"in_reply_to\": msg.get(\"subject\"), \"triage\": cls}, Tier.A2))\n83\t        res = self._dispatch(proposals)\n84\t        res.notes.append(f\"triaged {len(inbox.messages())} messages; {open_loops} open loop(s) drafted (awaiting approval)\")\n85\t        return res\n86\t\n87\t    # NOTE: there is intentionally no send()/transmit() method. Outbound is human-only, forever.\n88\t"}]},"uuid":"6bcd2380-150c-4506-b5e8-28aae100394e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:22.047Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"99c31e94-d898-4588-889c-4e5b2b8f1d22","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"5a8346bb-11cd-47a1-aec7-ae51e77f1ba0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA3UCvx1pVMQuG8u2xy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Let me read the promotion/no-promotion model, the approval queue, the classifier bridge, the scope gate, and the agent base."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":14237,"cache_read_input_tokens":16626,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":14237,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA3T4ii5td6QTgdLtjx","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"35ed8554-e097-4555-9076-bec88a3844b0","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:31.329Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6e18843f-5161-4bfe-a4ce-d977165031b6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FhDvH1vMQEHJnTVMEiGNAA","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scope promotion policy (SIGIL §5). An owner may promote a specific (agent, scope) so its A2\n2\tproposals auto-approve instead of queuing — EXCEPT ENVOY, which has NO promotion path (§4.6). A3\n3\tnever promotes.\n4\t\n5\tAUTHENTICATED (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2/RP-4): a grant/revoke is meaningless unless SIGNED by the owner\n6\tkey and verified against the persisted trusted pubkey. `is_promoted` ignores any grant that does not\n7\tverify — so a forged grant written by a prompt-injected agent via `self.store` grants nothing. The\n8\tgate scope is the proposal's RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label\n9\t(RP-3), so a promotion is \"agent X may auto-approve A2 records of kind K.\" \"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n15\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n16\t\n17\tSIGNAL = \"governor.promotion\"\n18\tNO_PROMOTION_AGENTS = frozenset({\"ENVOY\"})   # structural: outbound stays human-gated forever (§4.6)\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"agent\", \"scope\")\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass PromotionPolicy:\n23\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n24\t        self.store = store\n25\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n26\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n27\t\n28\t    def grant(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\"):\n29\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n30\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\",\n31\t                              payload={\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"agent\": agent,\n32\t                                       \"scope\": scope, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n33\t                                       \"reason\": f\"{agent} has no promotion path (SIGIL §4.6) — outbound stays human-gated\"})\n34\t            return None\n35\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"granted\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n36\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n37\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n38\t\n39\t    def revoke(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> int:\n40\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n41\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n42\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n43\t\n44\t    def is_promoted(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> bool:\n45\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n46\t            return False                          # never — structural, not policy\n47\t        state: dict = {}\n48\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n49\t            p = r.payload\n50\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL or p.get(\"state\") not in (\"granted\", \"revoked\"):\n51\t                continue\n52\t            if not verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n53\t                continue                          # fail-closed: an unsigned/forged grant grants nothing\n54\t            state[(p.get(\"agent\"), p.get(\"scope\"))] = p[\"state\"]   # latest verified wins\n55\t        return state.get((agent, scope)) == \"granted\" or state.get((agent, \"*\")) == \"granted\"\n56\t"}]},"uuid":"76a93e6a-266b-4ec7-8c01-ec093306525b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:32.264Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6e18843f-5161-4bfe-a4ce-d977165031b6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8d61f217-1f0f-4eb1-9d48-e0a25270afff","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N4sDVNH2MEtL5jeXSwtnMW","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"171e70fb-147b-4653-a535-88a55fa57a7a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:32.901Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8d61f217-1f0f-4eb1-9d48-e0a25270afff","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"797801db-55f8-43cc-b838-d8545422dd21","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MotLyEFDMueb2rKhyuE2Ar","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"KernelClassifier (Phase 7, P0.1) — the Python↔Rust WARDEN bridge. The authoritative danger\n2\tclassifier is Rust `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3),\n3\tcomposed with the raise-only registry pins in `Warden::decide`. Python's `Governor` otherwise\n4\tTRUSTS a Proposal's declared tier; this bridge lets the mesh DERIVE a tool's tier from that\n5\tfail-closed oracle instead of self-declaring it — so an Operator step or a Vision egress hop is\n6\ttiered by the same code that enforces at the kernel.\n7\t\n8\tConsulting the kernel is a pure A0 observation (`sigil-kernel classify` writes NO action-log\n9\trecord). Any failure — missing binary, timeout, non-zero exit, unparseable output, or an unknown\n10\ttier string — resolves to **A3** (fail-closed to the most-gated tier), mirroring the Rust\n11\tclassifier's own posture: never let a bridge error silently downgrade a dangerous tool.\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport json\n15\timport subprocess\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..voice.dispatch import _default_bin  # reuse the exact kernel-binary resolution\n18\tfrom .base import Tier\n19\t\n20\t_TIER = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass KernelClassifier:\n24\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n25\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n26\t        self.timeout = timeout\n27\t\n28\t    def classify(self, tool: str) -> Tier:\n29\t        \"\"\"Return the WARDEN tier for `tool`. Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\n30\t        if not tool or not tool.strip():\n31\t            return Tier.A3\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n36\t            return Tier.A3\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            return Tier.A3\n39\t        try:\n40\t            data = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])\n41\t            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\n42\t        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n43\t            return Tier.A3\n44\t"}]},"uuid":"f04b49d7-f78a-4be5-a501-ede2eb364d81","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:33.273Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"797801db-55f8-43cc-b838-d8545422dd21","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"74b95cb4-c42f-4ee6-9b3a-84e9c9ad4ed5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019of47pVntBdHdhgdGgxaHb","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"OperatorScope (Phase 7, WS-B B-ii) — the capability-scoped two-ring sandbox that bounds where the\n2\tOperator may read and auto-write. Wraps the currently-UNGUARDED local-path branch of\n3\t`sources.read_source` so the Operator can never touch an arbitrary path.\n4\t\n5\tTwo rings:\n6\t  • read_roots — the Operator may READ here (A0/A1).\n7\t  • auto_write_roots — the narrower set where an A1 reversible write may AUTO-apply; a write/delete\n8\t    inside a read root but OUTSIDE an auto-write root must be QUEUED for approval, not auto-run.\n9\t\n10\tEvery path is realpath-resolved (symlinks followed) and must be *within* an allowed root — a `..`,\n11\ta symlink escaping the root, or an absolute path elsewhere resolves outside and is refused. EMPTY\n12\troots = deny-all (fail-closed), the inverse of `read_source`'s current unguarded default. The\n13\tresolved real path is returned so the caller opens exactly what was vetted (no re-resolve → no\n14\tTOCTOU gap between the check and the open).\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _within(p: Path, root: Path) -> bool:\n22\t    return p == root or p.is_relative_to(root)\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tclass OperatorScope:\n26\t    def __init__(self, read_roots: Optional[List[str]] = None,\n27\t                 auto_write_roots: Optional[List[str]] = None):\n28\t        self.read_roots = [Path(r).resolve() for r in (read_roots or [])]\n29\t        self.auto_write_roots = [Path(r).resolve() for r in (auto_write_roots or [])]\n30\t\n31\t    @staticmethod\n32\t    def _realpath(path: str) -> Optional[Path]:\n33\t        \"\"\"Resolve symlinks + `..` (strict=False so a not-yet-existing write target still resolves\n34\t        via its existing parents). None on an unresolvable path.\"\"\"\n35\t        try:\n36\t            return Path(path).resolve()\n37\t        except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):\n38\t            return None\n39\t\n40\t    def resolve(self, path: str, need: str = \"read\") -> Optional[Path]:\n41\t        \"\"\"Return the vetted REAL path iff it is within the ring for `need` ('read' | 'write'), else\n42\t        None. Open THIS returned path (never re-resolve `path`) to avoid a check/open TOCTOU.\"\"\"\n43\t        rp = self._realpath(path)\n44\t        if rp is None:\n45\t            return None\n46\t        roots = self.auto_write_roots if need == \"write\" else self.read_roots\n47\t        return rp if any(_within(rp, root) for root in roots) else None\n48\t\n49\t    def in_read(self, path: str) -> bool:\n50\t        return self.resolve(path, \"read\") is not None\n51\t\n52\t    def in_auto_write(self, path: str) -> bool:\n53\t        return self.resolve(path, \"write\") is not None\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"9011fa11-3465-4adb-b9b6-4677f5272a55","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:33.864Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"74b95cb4-c42f-4ee6-9b3a-84e9c9ad4ed5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"88ecd324-57e0-43d1-a3ce-b43d1c7e2c6a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VfQMXM8C9ake15T3FDbAk8","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"a20f210e-39c3-426a-a464-b35bd5436af3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:34.033Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"88ecd324-57e0-43d1-a3ce-b43d1c7e2c6a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n98:/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\n99:const A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n104:/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n106:/// `ingest.status`). A3_TOKENS / A2_TOKENS / A0_VERBS / A0_TOOLS arrays =====\n/// A3 — destructive verbs, financial ops, crypto/restore ops, and DANGEROUS TARGETS\n/// (secret/identity/network/prod material is A3 regardless of the verb: `secrets.read`,\n/// `iam.policy.write`, `budget.get`, `vault.read`).\nconst A3_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n    // destructive verbs\n    \"push\", \"deploy\", \"delete\", \"destroy\", \"drop\", \"remove\", \"purge\", \"wipe\", \"truncate\",\n    \"overwrite\", \"erase\", \"format\", \"kill\", \"shutdown\", \"reboot\", \"reset\", \"disable\", \"enable\",\n    \"override\", \"force\", \"sudo\", \"exec\", \"eval\", \"chmod\", \"chown\", \"patch\", \"install\", \"uninstall\",\n    // crypto + restore/rollback (a restore silently reverts current state)\n    \"encrypt\", \"decrypt\", \"restore\", \"revert\", \"rollback\", \"recover\",\n    // financial\n    \"spend\", \"purchase\", \"pay\", \"payment\", \"transaction\", \"transfer\", \"refund\", \"invoice\",\n    \"allocate\", \"budget\", \"release\", \"sign\",\n    // security / identity / infra / secret-store TARGETS (dangerous no matter the verb)\n    \"secret\", \"secrets\", \"credential\", \"credentials\", \"token\", \"tokens\", \"key\", \"keys\", \"iam\",\n    \"policy\", \"firewall\", \"acl\", \"role\", \"grant\", \"revoke\", \"rotate\", \"escalate\", \"infra\",\n    \"prod\", \"production\", \"env\", \"master\", \"root\", \"admin\",\n    \"vault\", \"keyring\", \"keychain\", \"keystore\", \"hsm\",\n];\n\n/// A2 — external-visible / semi-reversible: communication, publishing, and bulk DATA EGRESS\n/// (export/dump of a private store must be human-seen, never auto).\nconst A2_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"send\", \"email\", \"smtp\", \"publish\", \"post\", \"message\", \"outbound\", \"webhook\", \"sms\", \"notify\",\n    \"share\", \"upload\", \"invite\", \"calendar\", \"tweet\", \"dm\", \"export\", \"dump\", \"download\", \"sync\",\n];\n\n/// A1 — reversible internal writes (drafts, notes, memory writes, non-push commits). NOTE:\n/// \"snapshot\" is intentionally NOT here — a snapshot RESTORE is destructive (caught by A3\n/// \"restore\"), and snapshot-create alone falls to fail-closed A3 rather than risk auto-run.\nconst A1_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"write\", \"note\", \"brief\", \"report\", \"draft\", \"alert\", \"consolidate\", \"commit\", \"branch\",\n    \"annotate\", \"tag\", \"label\",\n];\n\n/// A0 — POSITIVE allowlist of known-safe observe/answer VERBS only (NO target nouns — a target\n/// noun here would let e.g. `memory.encrypt` auto-run). A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\nconst A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"read\", \"search\", \"query\", \"get\", \"list\", \"status\", \"recall\", \"observe\", \"answer\",\n    \"view\", \"show\", \"find\", \"frame\", \"peek\", \"describe\", \"inspect\", \"lookup\",\n];\n\n/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n/// (`graph.entity`, `episodic.range`, `threads.open`, `commitments.due`, `contradictions.pending`,\n/// `ingest.status`). Matched on the whole lowercased tool name.\nconst A0_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n    \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n];\n\n/// Split a tool name into whole tokens on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/` and whitespace, lowercased.\nfn tokens(tool: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    tool.split(|c: char| c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '/' || c.is_whitespace())\n        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"01d17a76-2fa4-46d6-824b-9194d247ddbb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:14.294Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2ec789ac-ffe8-4268-a5ed-74a4860661f4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
