{"parentUuid":"4af923aa-ef27-4190-a122-f24579c6f6fa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Uzy4xYjvb2ofyko7HxnjFS","type":"tool_result","content":"===== test_operator: preview/approve/execute structure + doubles =====\n\"\"\"SIGIL Phase 7 WS-B — OPERATOR: scope sandbox, grounded reads, and the plan → preview → approve →\nexecute → verify → rollback/undo transaction. Run: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_operator.py\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom sigil.agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\nfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\nfrom sigil.agents.operator import Operator, Step\nfrom sigil.agents.operator_scope import OperatorScope\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nOWNER = generate_keypair()\nOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n\n\ndef _store():\n    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n\n\nclass FakeClassifier:\n    \"\"\"Deterministic stand-in for the Rust oracle (fs.read=A0, fs.write=A1, fs.delete/shell=A3).\"\"\"\n    def classify(self, tool):\n        if tool == \"fs.read\":\n            return Tier.A0\n        if tool == \"fs.write\":\n            return Tier.A1\n        return Tier.A3          # fs.delete, shell.exec.*, unknown → fail-closed A3\n\n\ndef _root():\n    return Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"op-root-\"))\n\n\ndef _op(store, root, *, auto_write=True):\n    scope = OperatorScope(read_roots=[str(root)], auto_write_roots=[str(root)] if auto_write else [])\n    return Operator(store, scope=scope, classifier=FakeClassifier(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n\n\n# ---- B2 scope sandbox ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef test_scope_refuses_outside_and_traversal():\n    root = _root()\n    sc = OperatorScope(read_roots=[str(root)])\n    (root / \"ok.txt\").write_text(\"hi\")\n    assert sc.resolve(str(root / \"ok.txt\"), \"read\") is not None, \"a path inside the root resolves\"\n    assert sc.resolve(str(root / \"..\" / \"etc\" / \"passwd\"), \"read\") is None, \"traversal out is refused\"\n    assert sc.resolve(\"/etc/passwd\", \"read\") is None, \"an absolute path outside is refused\"\n\n\ndef test_scope_symlink_escape_refused():\n    root = _root()\n    outside = _root()\n    (outside / \"secret\").write_text(\"SECRET\")\n    (root / \"link\").symlink_to(outside / \"secret\")     # a symlink escaping the root\n    sc = OperatorScope(read_roots=[str(root)])\n    assert sc.resolve(str(root / \"link\"), \"read\") is None, \"a symlink resolving outside the root is refused\"","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"eb11e74a-d7c6-406b-b670-cdedc5d8a64e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:35.125Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4af923aa-ef27-4190-a122-f24579c6f6fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"b7c4aa23-6171-48fa-a586-d43867fe0ca4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KoTyYZeB1RSKAseX8gqZk6","type":"tool_result","content":"===== vision.py: egress wiring =====\n5:FRONTIER model is a data-egress hop and is gated (see `perception.egress`).\n8:  • `MoondreamVision` — a local moondream-class VLM via Ollama. Fully offline, zero egress → A0.\n10:  • `ClaudeVision` — the frontier Anthropic vision API. `describe()` performs a REAL image UPLOAD\n11:    (private screen bytes leave the box), so it MUST only be invoked through `perception.egress`,\n12:    which classifies the upload A2 via the WARDEN oracle and requires a verified owner approval.\n29:    # `egresses` = does describe() send bytes off the box? On-box models (moondream) are False and may\n30:    # run on the auto A0 path; frontier models are True and may ONLY be invoked through the egress gate\n31:    # (`Perceptor.frontier`). `perceive`/`ambient_watch` STRUCTURALLY refuse an egressing model.\n32:    egresses: bool\n48:    egresses = False   # on-box — safe on the auto perceive/ambient path\n72:    \"\"\"FRONTIER Anthropic vision API — `describe()` UPLOADS the image (data egress). Gate it via\n73:    `perception.egress`; do not call directly on an auto path. Mirrors `extract.ApiProvider`.\"\"\"\n74:    egresses = True    # sends bytes off-box — perceive/ambient refuse it; only frontier() may use it\n\n===== full egress test block =====\n        assert MoondreamVision().describe(frame, \"what?\") == \"a red coffee mug\"\n    finally:\n        _vision_mod.urllib.request.urlopen = orig\n\n\n# ---- A2 frontier egress gate (THE doctrine: nothing leaves the box unapproved) -------------------\ndef test_frontier_queues_egress_and_uploads_nothing():\n    s = _store()\n    spy = SpyVision()\n    res = Perceptor(s).frontier(\"what is this?\", StaticFrame(text=\"secret dashboard\"),\n                                vision=spy, classifier=FakeClassifier(Tier.A2), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n    assert spy.calls == 0, \"NO image is uploaded before approval\"\n    assert res.queued and res.queued[0][\"tier\"] == \"A2\", \"the egress is A2-queued for approval\"\n    assert not res.applied\n\n\ndef test_frontier_uploads_only_after_verified_approval():\n    s = _store()\n    spy = SpyVision()\n    p = Perceptor(s)\n    frame = StaticFrame(text=\"secret dashboard\")\n    q = p.frontier(\"q\", frame, vision=spy, classifier=FakeClassifier(Tier.A2), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n    egress_seq = q.queued[0][\"seq\"]\n    assert spy.calls == 0\n    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(egress_seq)   # owner-signed\n    r = p.frontier(\"q\", frame, vision=spy, classifier=FakeClassifier(Tier.A2),\n                   trusted_pubkey=OP, approved_seq=egress_seq)\n    assert spy.calls == 1, \"the upload happens ONLY after a verified owner approval\"\n    assert r.applied, \"the frontier reading is served (advisory)\"\n    assert \"frontier\" in s.get(r.applied[0]).payload.get(\"source_model\", \"\")\n\n\ndef test_frontier_approval_is_bound_to_the_exact_egress():\n    s = _store()\n    spy = SpyVision()\n    p = Perceptor(s)\n    f1 = StaticFrame(text=\"benign wiki page\", tag=\"1\")\n    f2 = StaticFrame(text=\"SECRET private keys\", tag=\"2\")\n    seq1 = p.frontier(\"q\", f1, vision=spy, classifier=FakeClassifier(), trusted_pubkey=OP).queued[0][\"seq\"]\n    ApprovalQueue(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(seq1)\n    # replay seq1's approval to try to upload a DIFFERENT frame → token mismatch → refused (re-queued)\n    r = p.frontier(\"q\", f2, vision=spy, classifier=FakeClassifier(), trusted_pubkey=OP, approved_seq=seq1)\n    assert spy.calls == 0, \"an approval for egress A cannot authorize uploading frame B (bound by egress_token)\"\n    assert r.queued and not r.applied\n\n\ndef test_egress_approved_helper_fail_closed():\n    s = _store()\n    frame = StaticFrame(text=\"x\")\n    tok = egress_token(frame.sha256, \"q\")\n    # {"parentUuid":"f2fc301d-0eb2-44e7-9838-2bacac225938","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA8ndqpzk8ZGcEkfbaV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EVirCRafKqa1SBsc85ng5u","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"echo \"===== agents/__init__.py =====\" && cat /home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/__init__.py; echo; echo \"===== steward.py header =====\"; sed -n '1,20p' /home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/steward.py; echo; echo \"===== SIGIL.md C32/C33/4.6 refs (if present) =====\"; grep -rniE \"C32|C33|form-fill|browser automation|outbound payment|4\\.6|ENVOY\" /home/kali/sigil/SIGIL.md /home/kali/sigil/CLAUDE.md 2>/dev/null | head -20; echo \"--- doc files present? ---\"; ls /home/kali/sigil/*.md 2>/dev/null","description":"Check agent registry, steward, doctrine doc refs"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4566,"cache_read_input_tokens":52083,"output_tokens":5615,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4566},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":5615,"cache_read_input_tokens":52083,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4566,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4566,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA8kaZc7tE79HZBxMAG","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"5b0f445b-85f2-4e18-87f0-b2b6f8953570","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:33:03.882Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"5b0f445b-85f2-4e18-87f0-b2b6f8953570","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a7b30c6369923efac","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EVirCRafKqa1SBsc85ng5u","type":"tool_result","content":"===== agents/__init__.py =====\n\"\"\"SIGIL agent mesh (Phase 3, SIGIL §4) — ARCHIVIST, SENTINEL, STEWARD, ENVOY (drafts-only).\nEach agent has an autonomy ceiling and writes provenance-linked spine records; sensitive\nproposals queue for human approval and ENVOY outbound never auto-sends. Offense-free by\ndoctrine (assert_no_offense at import) — BASTION monitors own infrastructure only (Phase 5).\"\"\"\nfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n\nassert_no_offense()\n\nfrom .archivist import Archivist  # noqa: E402\nfrom .artificer import Artificer  # noqa: E402\nfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier  # noqa: E402\nfrom .bastion import Asset, Bastion  # noqa: E402\nfrom .envoy import Envoy, FileInbox  # noqa: E402\nfrom .operator import Operator, Step  # noqa: E402\nfrom .operator_scope import OperatorScope  # noqa: E402\nfrom .scholar import Scholar  # noqa: E402\nfrom .sentinel import Sentinel  # noqa: E402\nfrom .steward import Steward  # noqa: E402\n\n__all__ = [\"Agent\", \"AgentResult\", \"Proposal\", \"Tier\",\n           \"Archivist\", \"Sentinel\", \"Steward\", \"Envoy\", \"FileInbox\", \"Artificer\", \"Scholar\",\n           \"Bastion\", \"Asset\", \"Operator\", \"OperatorScope\", \"Step\"]\n\n===== steward.py header =====\n\"\"\"STEWARD (SIGIL §4.7) — personal operations: the morning brief, the commitment ledger,\nrecurring admin. Ceiling A2 (calendar writes queue until trust promotes them). Every line is cited to a spine seq.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom typing import List, Optional\n\nfrom ..consolidate.queries import due_commitments, open_threads, pending_contradictions\nfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n\n\ndef _recent_activity(store, since_seq: int) -> Counter:\n    c: Counter = Counter()\n    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=since_seq):\n        c[r.kind] += 1\n    return c\n\n\n===== SIGIL.md C32/C33/4.6 refs (if present) =====\n--- doc files present? ---\n/home/kali/sigil/README.md","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"e1d9274f-8afd-4a27-95f6-fb98522b2fb6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:33:07.139Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5b0f445b-85f2-4e18-87f0-b2b6f8953570","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Here is the design.\n\n---\n\n# SIGIL Workstream: OWNER-CONSENTED Identity Manager + Autonomous Web ACTOR (\"DELEGATE\")\n\n## 0. Verified anchor facts (from reading the code, not the brief)\n\nThree load-bearing facts I confirmed by reading the source, because the whole design leans on them:\n\n- **Honest tool names already fail-closed to A3.** `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (lines 122-141) is danger-first, then A2, then A1, then A0 *only* via a positive verb allowlist (`A0_VERBS`, lines 99-102 = `read, search, query, get, list, status, recall, observe, answer, view, show, find, frame, peek, describe, inspect, lookup`). `fetch`, `create`, `login`, `fill`, `submit`, `navigate`, `autofill` are **in no set** → fail-closed A3. So `account.create`, `login`, `form.fill`, `submit.form`, `browser.navigate`, `password.autofill` all classify **A3**; `credential.read`/`purchase` hit explicit A3 tokens; `http.get` is A0 (verb `get`); `web.fetch`/`scrape.fetch` are A3 (verb `fetch` absent); `message.send`/`email.send` are A2. **The naming honesty lever works with zero kernel changes.**\n- **The egress gate is a 15-line, per-action, replay-proof template** (`perception/egress.py`): `egress_token = sha256(subject|bound-fields)`, and `egress_approved` requires a `verify_approval`-verified owner/device approval whose `target_seq` *is* the request bound to that token. Wrong token / different seq / unsigned → False. This is the exact shape the ACTOR needs, per-action.\n- **Operator already keeps bytes off the spine** (`operator.py` 205-212): executable plan *with content* → `0700 _PLANS/{hash}.json`; the spine record is REDACTED to hashes only (209-212). Execute re-derives tier from the hash-bound preview (259-263) and aborts on any hash drift (251-258, anti-TOCTOU). That is the discipline the credential vault and the ACTOR both inherit.\n\nAgent name chosen: **DELEGATE** (distinct from ENVOY/STEWARD; \"acts by owner delegation, per-action approved\"). It is added to `NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS` alongside ENVOY.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Peak-innovative mechanisms (doctrine-safe)\n\n### (a) Per-service CREDENTIAL VAULT extending SecretStore\n\nA typed layer over `SecretStore` (`platform/secrets.py`), preserving its invariant verbatim (\"Secrets NEVER enter the append-only spine, a log, or a network payload\", secrets.py:4-6).\n\n- **Record type** `VaultRecord{service, email, username, password_ref, notes, version}`. Note there is **no `password` field** — only `password_ref`, a keyring key name (e.g. `vault/github/password`). `version` is a monotonic counter bumped on every edit (used for approval binding — see below).\n- **The secret lives in the keyring.** `vault.set_record(...)` calls `SecretStore.set(password_ref, value)` (secrets.py:39-51: keyring-first, else the 0600 `sigil.env` written via `os.open(...,0o600)` up-front, secrets.py:70). The value is *never* returned to a caller except at execute-time resolution.\n- **The manifest carries NO passwords and lives OFF the spine.** `{service → {email, username, password_ref, notes, version}}` is written to a 0700 directory `SIGIL_HOME/\"vault\"/manifest.json`, created with the exact `operator._secure` (0700 dir, operator.py:45-51) + `os.open(...,0o600)` (secrets.py:70) discipline. The append-only spine **never** receives the manifest — only, when an action references a record, a `{service, vault_ref, version}` triple and a decision.\n- **Resolution only at execute time.** `vault.resolve_password(record)` → `SecretStore.get(password_ref)` is called *inside* `DELEGATE.execute()` at the moment of fill, from the keyring, into a local variable, into the POST body / browser fill — and is never assigned to a Proposal payload, never logged, never journaled. This mirrors the 0700-journal split: the *plan* knows which ref to resolve; the *value* is fetched fresh from the keyring at the last instant.\n\n### (b) WEB ACTOR: plan → preview(by reference) → per-action A3 → execute → verify → surface\n\n`DELEGATE` fuses the Operator transaction shape with the egress per-action token:\n\n1. **PLAN** a task into an ordered list of `WebStep{kind, url, fields}` where `kind ∈ {navigate, fill, submit, account.create, login, purchase}` and `fields` are **vault references** (`email ← vault:github#email`, `password ← vault:github#password_ref@v3`) or owner-typed literals — never raw secret values.\n2. **PREVIEW** exactly like `operator.preview` (operator.py:194-224): an **A0 dry-run** `http.get` of each target page (SSRF-gated, no mutation), showing which fields will be filled *by reference* and a diff-style manifest. The executable step (with vault refs) is journaled to a 0700 dir; the spine record is redacted to `{service, step_kind, url_host, page_sha256, fields:[{name, vault_ref, version}], action_token}` — **no values**. Each mutating step is queued as **its own A3 proposal** (not one blanket plan approval — this is the ENVOY/egress \"per-action\" upgrade over Operator's single plan-hash approval).\n3. **APPROVE** — per step, owner-signed via `ApprovalQueue.approve` (approvals.py:105), bound to that step's `action_token`.\n4. **EXECUTE** withholds until `action_approved(store, step_seq, action_token, owner_pubkey)` returns True (mirror of `egress_approved`). At execute it **re-fetches** the page, **re-derives** the `action_token`, and aborts on mismatch (page changed OR vault version bumped) — anti-TOCTOU, mirroring operator.py:251-263. Only then does it resolve the password from the keyring and drive the engine.\n5. **VERIFY** by re-reading the world (post-submit page: presence of a logged-in marker / account-created confirmation), recorded as an A1 outcome — never the credential.\n6. **SURFACE** on any block signal (see (d)).\n\n### (c) HTTP-FIRST engine on read_source's foundation, browser as lazy fallback\n\n- **`WebEngine` Protocol** (like `envoy.InboxSource`, runtime_checkable): `fetch(url) -> PageView` and `act(step, resolved) -> ActResult`.\n- **`HttpEngine`** reuses `sources.py` wholesale: `_vetted_ip` (sources.py:31-57), `_PinnedHTTPHandler`/`_PinnedHTTPSHandler` (64-115, IP pinned but SNI/cert on the real host), `_NoRedirect` (118-121), and the correlatable UA (sources.py:135). GET for preview; `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` POST for simple static-form submits (built as a raw `urllib.request.Request` in the shape of `consolidate/extract.py:157-167`).\n- **`BrowserEngine`** lazy-imports Playwright **only when a page needs JS** (see §5), behind the same Protocol. Locked-down context: same correlatable UA, navigation restricted to `ActorScope` origins, downloads disabled, no remote-debug port, **no stealth/proxy/UA-rotation args**.\n- **`FakeEngine`** — the deterministic test double + call-spy, driving a local `http.server` with a fake signup/login form.\n\n### (d) Structural offense-free guards\n\n- **NO_PROMOTION identity agent.** Add `\"DELEGATE\"` to `NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS` (promotion.py:18). `is_promoted` returns False structurally (promotion.py:45-46); A3 never promotes regardless. So no A2 read/notify step and no A3 mutation can ever be auto-promoted.\n- **\"Bot-block = positive control\" detector.** `detect_block(status, html)` flags `403/429`, reCAPTCHA/hCaptcha/Turnstile script srcs, Cloudflare `cf-chl`/\"Just a moment\"/\"Attention Required\", and generic `captcha` tokens. On a hit, `DELEGATE` **STOPS**, writes a positive-control finding (`signal=\"web.actor.blocked\", decision=\"surfaced\"`), and returns. There is **no** solver, proxy, header-spoof, or UA-rotation code path anywhere — the absence is the guarantee. The block-check runs **before** the escalation check, so \"escalate to browser to get past a block\" is structurally impossible.\n- **Per-service creation CAP.** `ActorScope.creation_cap` (default **1**). Before any `account.create`, count APPLIED `account.create` steps for that service on the spine; `>= cap` → refuse as a positive control (\"mass account creation is out of doctrine\"). Checked at preview AND re-checked at execute.\n- **Owner-identity-only.** The vault has no arbitrary-identity field and `DELEGATE` has **no `as_identity`/`impersonate` parameter**. Fields resolve *only* from the owner's own `VaultRecord`. Signup uses the owner's own email only. Impersonation is prevented by the *absence* of a code path, not a check.\n- **ActorScope origin allowlist** (mirror `OperatorScope`, empty = deny-all): the owner declares which service origins are THEIR OWN integrations; a step targeting anything else is refused at preview. Combined with the SSRF gate (no internal hosts) this enforces CLAUDE.md §II \"test only the owner's integration.\"\n\n### (e) Every credential-touching action keeps secrets off-spine\n\nThe spine ever only sees, per action: `signal, service, step_kind, url_host, page_sha256, fields:[{name, vault_ref, version}], action_token, tier, governor-decision, gated outcome`. Binding is by **`vault_ref + version`**, deliberately **not** a hash of the value — hashing a low-entropy identity field (email/username) onto an append-only log is itself a weak-preimage leak a reviewer would flag. Version-binding gives anti-TOCTOU (rotate password → version bump → token mismatch → re-approval) with **zero** secret-derived bytes on the spine. Owner-typed *literal* form values (a bio, a display preference) are treated like Operator file content: journaled 0700, spine gets a `content_sha256` only.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Ordered, independently-mergeable slices\n\n**Slice 1 — Credential vault (foundation).**\nCREATE `sigil/agents/vault.py`: `VaultRecord`, `CredentialVault(secret_store=None)` with `set_record/get_record/records/resolve_password/bump_version`. Reuse: `platform/secrets.py` `SecretStore.set/get` (39-51) for the password; `config.SIGIL_HOME`; `operator._secure` (45-51) + `os.open(...,0o600)` (secrets.py:70) for the 0700 manifest. No spine writes of values. Independently testable.\n\n**Slice 2 — WARDEN honesty invariant (test-only; reuse-first, no kernel change).**\nCREATE a test asserting `KernelClassifier.classify` gives A3 for `account.create/login/submit.form/form.fill/browser.navigate/password.autofill/purchase/web.fetch`, A0 for `http.get`, A2 for `message.send`. (OPTIONAL, only for readability, raise-only: add `\"password\",\"signup\",\"captcha\",\"autofill\"` as explicit A3 pins in `kernel/src/tiers.rs` — but the default is to change nothing since fail-closed already covers it.)\n\n**Slice 3 — Action gate.**\nCREATE `sigil/agents/actor_gate.py`: `action_token(service, step_kind, page_sha256, field_binding)` and `action_approved(store, seq, token, trusted_pubkey)` — a near-verbatim mirror of `perception/egress.py:25-44` with `signal=\"web.actor.step\"`. Reuse: `approvals.verify_approval` (33-50), `mesh.authorized_devices` (registry.py:63), `reuse.sha256_hex`. Independently testable against a stub store.\n\n**Slice 4 — Actor scope + caps.**\nCREATE `sigil/agents/actor_scope.py`: `ActorScope(allowed_origins, creation_cap=1)` with `origin_allowed(url)` (scheme+host allowlist, empty=deny-all) and `creation_allowed(store, service)`. Mirror `operator_scope.py` (deny-all default 40-47). Reuse: `sources._vetted_ip` (public-only) + `urlsplit`. Independently testable.\n\n**Slice 5 — Web engine + block detector + escalation.**\nCREATE `sigil/agents/web_engine.py`: `WebEngine` Protocol, `PageView`, `HttpEngine` (reuse all of `sources.py`: `_vetted_ip`, `_PinnedHTTP(S)Handler`, `_NoRedirect`, UA at 135), `detect_block`, `needs_js`, `BrowserEngine` (lazy Playwright, locked-down), `FakeEngine` (spy). Independently testable with a local `http.server`.\n\n**Slice 6 — The DELEGATE agent (integration).**\nCREATE `sigil/agents/actor.py`: `DELEGATE(Agent)`, `ceiling=A2`, methods `plan/preview/execute/verify`. Mirror `operator.py` (preview no-mutation → 0700 journal → redacted spine record; execute re-derives token + checks `action_approved` + resolves keyring last-instant + verifies) and `egress.py` (per-action token). MODIFY `sigil/governor/promotion.py:18` add `\"DELEGATE\"`. MODIFY `sigil/agents/__init__.py` to export. Reuse: `base.Agent._dispatch` (still routes through the Governor kill-switch/budget, base.py:71-100).\n\n**Slice 7 — Tests + real proof.**\nCREATE `tests/test_actor.py` (see §3). Reuse `test_operator.py`/`test_vision.py` patterns (owner keypair, `_store`, `FakeClassifier`, spy).\n\nSlices 1–5 are independently mergeable; 6 depends on 1/3/4/5; 7 depends on 6.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Test + real-proof strategy\n\n**Deterministic doubles.**\n- **Local test HTTP server** (stdlib `http.server`, mirroring the `read_source` loopback tests at test_hardening.py:202-210): routes for `/signup` (fake form), `/login` (fake form), `/captcha` (returns a Turnstile/`cf-chl` page), `/blocked` (returns 403), `/js-app` (empty body + bundle `<script>` → needs-JS). Because the ACTOR pins the resolved IP and refuses internal hosts, tests bind the server and target it through a `FakeEngine`/loopback-permitting test seam so the SSRF gate is exercised, not bypassed silently.\n- **`FakeEngine` spy** records every `fetch`/`act` call (like `SpyVision`, test_vision.py:82-102).\n- **Deterministic `WebEngine` double** for the block/escalation branches.\n\n**Assertions (the doctrine, as tests).**\n1. **Secrets never on spine/log.** Set a vault password to a unique sentinel, run a full plan+approve+execute; scan the entire spine JSONL bytes and every emitted log line — the sentinel must appear **zero** times. Assert the spine record for the fill step contains `vault_ref`+`version` but no value and no value-hash.\n2. **Unapproved ⇒ no request.** Preview + queue, then call `execute` with no approval → `FakeEngine.calls == 0` and result is queued/withheld (mirror test_vision.py:66-73).\n3. **Executes only after a verified owner approval** (mirror test_vision.py:75-88): `ApprovalQueue.approve(step_seq)` owner-signed, then `execute` → exactly one `act` call, verify-marker recorded.\n4. **CAPTCHA/403 STOPS + surfaces.** Point a step at `/captcha` and `/blocked` → `act` performs no submit, a `web.actor.blocked` finding is written, no bypass attempt, UA unchanged.\n5. **Replay across actions/services refused** (mirror test_vision.py:92-102): approve action A, replay that approval onto action B (different `action_token`) → `calls == 0`, re-queued.\n6. **Creation cap.** Two `account.create` for one service with `cap=1` → the second refused at preview and at execute.\n7. **No identity field.** Assert `DELEGATE.plan`/`preview`/`execute` signatures expose no arbitrary-identity parameter and fields resolve only from the vault (a construction test, not a runtime check).\n8. **Escalation honesty.** `/blocked` routes to STOP, **never** to `BrowserEngine`; `/js-app` (no block signal) routes to `BrowserEngine`; assert the block-check precedes the needs-JS check.\n9. **Anti-TOCTOU.** Bump the vault `version` (or mutate the page) after approval → `execute` aborts with a hash/token-mismatch refusal, no `act` call.\n10. **No-stealth invariant.** Assert `BrowserEngine`'s launch args contain no proxy/stealth/UA-rotation and the UA equals the constant.\n\n**Red-pen lenses** (run explicitly): credential leak to spine / log / UA / Referer / journal; an action firing without a verified approval; approval replay across actions/services; evasion drift (any solver/proxy/UA-rotation/stealth); third-party attack (origin outside ActorScope, or SSRF to internal); browser provider exfiltrating (remote-debug port, cross-origin nav, downloads); impersonation via an identity field. Recommend running the `adversarial-sweep` skill on the finished stream (its brief notes it caught defects past a RED-PEN PASS).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Doctrine-safety analysis — abuse vectors and structural prevention\n\n| Vector a reviewer will hunt | Structural prevention |\n|---|---|\n| Password reaches the spine | Value only in keyring; manifest 0700 off-spine; spine binds by `vault_ref+version`; resolution is a last-instant local var. Test 1. |\n| Password in a log / UA / Referer | No value logging; UA is a compile-time constant (sources.py:135); Referer only owner-origin; credentials in POST body only. Tests 1, 10. |\n| Action fires with no approval | `execute` withholds until `action_approved` (egress mirror); DELEGATE ∈ `NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS`; A3 never auto (base.py `_dispatch`). Test 2. |\n| Approval replay across actions/services | `action_token` binds service+step+page+`vault-version`; `target_seq` binding (egress.py:41). Test 5. |\n| Forged / self-certified approval | `verify_approval` pins the *persisted* owner pubkey + owner-minted device set (approvals.py:33-50); unsigned/foreign key → False. |\n| Evasion drift (CAPTCHA solver / proxy / UA rotation) | No such code path exists; block-check precedes escalate; UA constant; `assert_no_offense()` blocks `framework.*`. Tests 4, 8, 10. |\n| Third-party attack | ActorScope origin allowlist (empty=deny-all) + SSRF `_vetted_ip` (no internal) + no cross-origin nav. |\n| Browser provider exfiltrates | Lazy-import, local-only, locked-down context, no remote-debug port, downloads off, nav restricted to ActorScope, values from keyring at fill-time only. |\n| Impersonation via identity field | No identity/impersonate parameter; fills only from owner's own vault. Test 7. |\n| Mass account creation | Per-service `creation_cap` default 1, preview + execute. Test 6. |\n| Page/vault TOCTOU after approval | Execute re-derives `action_token`; mismatch aborts (operator.py:251-263 mirror). Test 9. |\n| Global kill-switch/budget bypass | `_dispatch` still routes every proposal through the Governor. |\n\n---\n\n## 5. HTTP-first / browser-fallback escalation — how it is decided and kept honest\n\n**Decision tree, in strict order (order is the safety property):**\n1. `HttpEngine.fetch(url)` — SSRF-gated A0 GET.\n2. **Block-check first:** `detect_block(status, html)` → if a block signal (403/429/CAPTCHA/CF-challenge) → **STOP + surface as positive control.** Never escalate.\n3. **Needs-JS check second:** only if *not* blocked, `needs_js(html, wanted_fields)` is true when the fields the plan needs are absent from server-rendered HTML but referenced by a script, or the body is a JS-app shell, or a JS/meta redirect. → escalate to `BrowserEngine`.\n4. Otherwise stay on HTTP.\n\n**Kept honest by construction:**\n- A **block signal can never route to escalation** because step 2 precedes step 3 and returns. So \"use the browser to defeat the block\" is unreachable.\n- **`needs_js` is a missing-capability signal, not a block signal** — the two predicates are disjoint and independently tested (test 8).\n- Every escalation writes a spine finding (`signal=\"web.actor.escalate\", from=http, to=browser, reason`) — auditable.\n- Escalation changes **nothing** about posture: `BrowserEngine` uses the same correlatable UA, the same SSRF/ActorScope origin restriction, the same per-action approval, the same block-detector, and no stealth/proxy. The browser is a *rendering* fallback for the owner's own JS-heavy integration, not an *evasion* fallback.\n\n---\n\n### Critical Files for Implementation\n- /home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/secrets.py — SecretStore the vault extends (keyring + 0600 invariant, lines 4-6, 39-72)\n- /home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/operator.py — the plan→preview→approve→execute→verify + 0700-journal/off-spine template to mirror (45-51, 194-263)\n- /home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/egress.py — the per-action token + `*_approved` gate to mirror verbatim (25-44)\n- /home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/sources.py — the SSRF-gated, IP-pinned, correlatable-UA HTTP foundation the engine reuses (31-141)\n- /home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/promotion.py — NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS, where DELEGATE is registered (line 18, 44-55)\n\nSupporting: /home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/approvals.py (verify_approval), /home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/operator_scope.py (deny-all scope model), /home/kali/sigil/kernel/src/tiers.rs (the fail-closed classifier that makes honest names A3), /home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/base.py (Governor dispatch/ceilings).\n\nNote: I did not write a plan file — per my role I return the plan here as text for the parent agent.
